Sinatra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frank_Sinatra "Frank Sinatra"), [Dinah Shore](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dinah_Shore "Dinah Shore"), and [Gale Storm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gale_Storm "Gale Storm"),[[12]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LATabout-12) and several have been honored in two,[[34]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-people-36) including [Ron Howard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ron_Howard "Ron Howard"), who was the first to receive an additional star in more than twenty years when he received his second in 2015.[[139]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-144) Additionally, [Michael Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson "Michael Jackson"), [Diana Ross](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diana_Ross "Diana Ross"), [Smokey Robinson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Smokey_Robinson "Smokey Robinson"), and each member of [The Beatles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Beatles "The Beatles") have been honored twice in the same category, individually and as part of a group.[[140]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-145)[[141]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-146)
The family with the most individuals recognized is [the Barrymores](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Barrymores "The Barrymores"), with six.[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4) Other families with three or more individuals recognized include the [Bridges](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lloyd_Bridges "Lloyd Bridges"),[[142]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-147) [Carradines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carradine_family "Carradine family"),[[143]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-148)[[144]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-149) [Dern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bruce_Dern "Bruce Dern")-[Ladds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diane_Ladd "Diane Ladd"),[[145]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-150) [Garland](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judy_Garland "Judy Garland")-[Minnellis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vincente_Minnelli "Vincente Minnelli"),[[146]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-151)[[147]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-152) [Hustons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walter_Huston "Walter Huston"),[[148]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-153)[[149]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-154) [Lockharts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gene_Lockhart "Gene Lockhart"),[[150]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-155)[[151]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-156) and [Nelsons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ozzie_Nelson "Ozzie Nelson").[[152]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-157)
### Unique and unusual
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=20 "Edit section: Unique and unusual")]
#### Professions
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=21 "Edit section: Professions")]
Several [below-the-line filmmakers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Below-the-line_\(filmmaking\) "Below-the-line \(filmmaking\)") have been included in the Walk, including the following: [Conrad Hall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conrad_Hall "Conrad Hall"), [Leon Shamroy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leon_Shamroy "Leon Shamroy"), [Hal Mohr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hal_Mohr "Hal Mohr"), and [Haskell Wexler](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haskell_Wexler "Haskell Wexler") ([cinematographers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cinematographers "Cinematographers"));[[153]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-158) [Edith Head](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edith_Head "Edith Head") and [Ruth E. Carter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ruth_E._Carter "Ruth E. Carter") ([costume designers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Costume_designers "Costume designers"));[[154]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-159) [Yakima Canutt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yakima_Canutt "Yakima Canutt") ([stuntman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stuntman "Stuntman"));[[102]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-mildside-107) [Walter Lantz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walter_Lantz "Walter Lantz") ([animator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Animator "Animator"));[[102]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-mildside-107) [Rick Baker](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rick_Baker "Rick Baker"), [John Chambers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Chambers_\(make-up_artist\) "John Chambers \(make-up artist\)"), [Max Factor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Max_Factor_Sr. "Max Factor Sr."), and [The Westmores](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Westmore_family "Westmore family") ([makeup artists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Makeup_artists "Makeup artists"));[[155]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-maxfactor-160)[[156]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-161)[[157]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-162)[[158]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-163) and [Ray Harryhausen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ray_Harryhausen "Ray Harryhausen"), [Dennis Muren](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dennis_Muren "Dennis Muren"), and [Stan Winston](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stan_Winston "Stan Winston") ([visual effects](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Visual_effects "Visual effects"), [special effects](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special_effects "Special effects"), and [special makeup effects](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special_makeup_effects "Special makeup effects") artists).[[159]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-164)[[160]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-165)[[161]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-166) Max Factor was a makeup manufacturer as well.[[155]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-maxfactor-160)
Novelists are not covered by the Walk;[[162]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-novel-167) however, some novelists have stars, including [Sidney Sheldon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sidney_Sheldon "Sidney Sheldon"), who wrote screenplays before becoming a novelist;[[162]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-novel-167) [Ray Bradbury](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ray_Bradbury "Ray Bradbury"), whose work has been adapted into numerous movies and television programs;[[163]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-168) and [Harold Robbins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harold_Robbins "Harold Robbins"), who also had experience in the film industry.[[164]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-169)[[165]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-170) Film critic [Roger Ebert](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Ebert "Roger Ebert") has a star,[[166]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-171) although it is in the television category as his review shows were televised.[[167]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-172) Movie theater proprietors are also included, most notably [Sid Grauman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sid_Grauman "Sid Grauman"),[[31]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Q&A-33) whose theaters included the [Chinese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grauman's_Chinese_Theatre "Grauman's Chinese Theatre"), [Egyptian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grauman's_Egyptian_Theatre "Grauman's Egyptian Theatre"), [Million Dollar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Million_Dollar_Theater "Million Dollar Theater"), and [Rialto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rialto_Theater_\(Los_Angeles\) "Rialto Theater \(Los Angeles\)"),[[168]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-173) and [Earvin Magic Johnson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Earvin_Magic_Johnson "Earvin Magic Johnson"), former owner of [Magic Johnson Theaters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Magic_Johnson_Theaters "Magic Johnson Theaters").[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4)[[169]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-174)
Numerous inventors have stars on the Walk,[[170]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-175) including the following: [George Eastman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Eastman "George Eastman"), inventor of roll film;[[171]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-176) [Herbert Kalmus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Herbert_Kalmus "Herbert Kalmus"), co-developer of [color film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Color_film "Color film");[[172]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-177) [Thomas Edison](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Edison "Thomas Edison"), developer of many devices used in the film industry, including the [phonograph](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Phonograph "Phonograph"), [motion picture camera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Motion_picture_camera "Motion picture camera"), and long-lasting, practical [electric light bulb](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electric_light_bulb "Electric light bulb");[[173]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-178) [Auguste and Louis Lumière](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Auguste_and_Louis_Lumi%C3%A8re "Auguste and Louis Lumière"), developers of several motion picture-related machines including cameras and [projectors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Movie_projector "Movie projector");[[174]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-auguste-179)[[175]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-180) [William Selig](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Selig "William Selig"), inventor of the [Selig Standard Camera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selig_Standard_Camera "Selig Standard Camera") and [Selig Polyscope](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selig_Polyscope "Selig Polyscope");[[176]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-181) [Sigmund Lubin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sigmund_Lubin "Sigmund Lubin"), developer of his own motion picture camera and projector;[[177]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-182) [Jerry Fairbanks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jerry_Fairbanks "Jerry Fairbanks"), developer of [television's three camera system](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiple-camera_setup#Television "Multiple-camera setup") and co-developer of the [Zoomar lens](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zoomar_lens "Zoomar lens");[[178]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-183) [Lee de Forest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lee_de_Forest "Lee de Forest"), inventor of the [audion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Audion "Audion") and [phonofilm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Phonofilm "Phonofilm");[[179]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-184)[[180]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-185) [Ray Dolby](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ray_Dolby "Ray Dolby"), a pioneer in [audio technology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Audio_technology "Audio technology") and chief electronics designer for [Quadruplex videotape](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quadruplex_videotape "Quadruplex videotape");[[181]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-186)[[182]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-187) [Merian C. Cooper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Merian_C._Cooper "Merian C. Cooper"), co-developer of the [Cinerama process](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cinerama "Cinerama");[[183]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-188) and [Mark Serrurier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_Serrurier "Mark Serrurier"), who re-designed [Moviola](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moviola "Moviola"), which was invented by [his father](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iwan_Serrurier "Iwan Serrurier").[[184]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-189) [Hedy Lamarr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hedy_Lamarr "Hedy Lamarr"), co-inventor of a [frequency-hopping spread spectrum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frequency-hopping_spread_spectrum "Frequency-hopping spread spectrum") system that was further developed for use in [military communication](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_communication "Military communication") and [wireless technology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wireless_technology "Wireless technology"),[[185]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Lamarr-190)[[186]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-191) is also honored but for her acting work.[[187]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-192)
Several honorees have achieved political notability.[[188]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-193) [Presidents](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presidents_of_the_United_States "Presidents of the United States") [Ronald Reagan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ronald_Reagan "Ronald Reagan") and [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump") have stars,[[189]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-194)[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195) and Reagan is also one of two [California governors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Governor_of_California "Governor of California") with a star; the other is [Arnold Schwarzenegger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arnold_Schwarzenegger "Arnold Schwarzenegger").[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195)[[191]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-196) Former [U.S. Senator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._Senator "U.S. Senator") [George Murphy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Murphy "George Murphy")[[192]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-197)[[193]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-198) and [Representatives](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._House_of_Representatives "U.S. House of Representatives") [Helen Gahagan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Helen_Gahagan_Douglas "Helen Gahagan Douglas")[[194]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-199) and [Sonny Bono](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sonny_Bono "Sonny Bono")[[195]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-200) have stars, as does former [Prime Minister of Poland](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Poland "Prime Minister of Poland") [Ignacy Paderewski](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ignacy_Jan_Paderewski "Ignacy Jan Paderewski").[[196]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-201)
[Judges](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judge "Judge") have also received stars, including [Judge Greg Mathis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judge_Greg_Mathis "Judge Greg Mathis"), [Judge Judy Sheindlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judge_Judy_Sheindlin "Judge Judy Sheindlin"), and [Judge Joseph Wapner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judge_Joseph_Wapner "Judge Joseph Wapner"). These stars are all in the television category, as the judges' courts were televised.[[197]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-202)[[198]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-203)[[199]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-204)
#### Animals
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=22 "Edit section: Animals")]
[![A standard Walk of Fame star featuring the name Rin-Tin-Tin and a motion picture emblem](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b2/Rin-Tin-Tin_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame.jpg/250px-Rin-Tin-Tin_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Rin-Tin-Tin_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame.jpg)[Rin-Tin-Tin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rin-Tin-Tin "Rin-Tin-Tin")'s star, given for his work in motion pictures
Three dogs are included in the Walk:[[200]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-205) the fictional character [Lassie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lassie "Lassie")[[201]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-lassiehcoc-206) and animal actors [Rin Tin Tin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rin_Tin_Tin "Rin Tin Tin")[[202]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-207) and [Strongheart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Strongheart "Strongheart").[[203]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-208) An animated dog, [Snoopy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snoopy "Snoopy"), is also included, as are several other animated animals.[[204]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-209)
#### Fictional characters
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=23 "Edit section: Fictional characters")]
Main article: [List of fictional characters with stars on the Hollywood Walk of Fame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_fictional_characters_with_stars_on_the_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame "List of fictional characters with stars on the Hollywood Walk of Fame")
In addition to members of the entertainment industry, the Walk also honors fictional characters, with more than twenty honored as of 2024[[update]](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit)[[205]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-coleman-210)
The first fictional characters honored were [Lassie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lassie "Lassie") and [Betty Lou](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tommy_Riggs_and_Betty_Lou "Tommy Riggs and Betty Lou"); both were included when the Walk was unveiled in 1960.[[201]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-lassiehcoc-206)[[206]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trbl-211) [Mickey Mouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mickey_Mouse "Mickey Mouse"), the first fictional character added after the Walk's original unveiling, was added in 1978 in honor of his 50th anniversary.[[207]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-212)[[208]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-17fictional-213)[[Note 6]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-218) [Minnie Mouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minnie_Mouse "Minnie Mouse") was honored forty years later; when she was, her star was located directly outside [El Capitan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/El_Capitan_Theater "El Capitan Theater"),[[213]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-219) [Disney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disney "Disney")'s flagship theater.[[214]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-220) Numerous other animated characters have been added during anniversary years, including [Bugs Bunny](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bugs_Bunny "Bugs Bunny") on his 45th (in 1985), [The Simpsons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Simpson_family "Simpson family") and [Rugrats](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rugrats "Rugrats") on their 10th (2000 and 2001, respectively), [Shrek](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shrek_\(character\) "Shrek \(character\)") on his 20th (2010), [Snoopy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snoopy "Snoopy") on his 65th (2015), and [Alvin and the Chipmunks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alvin_and_the_Chipmunks "Alvin and the Chipmunks") on their 60th (2019);[[205]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-coleman-210)[[208]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-17fictional-213) [Godzilla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Godzilla "Godzilla") was also honored on his 50th anniversary, in 2004.[[215]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-221) In 1987, [Snow White](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snow_White_\(Disney_character\) "Snow White \(Disney character\)") became the first [Disney princess](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disney_princess "Disney princess") honored and in 2024, [Batman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Batman "Batman") became the first superhero honored.[[205]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-coleman-210)
[Clayton Moore](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clayton_Moore "Clayton Moore"), whose name is considered synonymous with the [Lone Ranger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lone_Ranger "Lone Ranger"),[[216]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-222) was honored with a star that includes both his name and the character.[[99]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fromthearchives-104) [Tommy Riggs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tommy_Riggs_and_Betty_Lou "Tommy Riggs and Betty Lou") is also listed alongside Betty Lou, a character he voiced.[[206]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trbl-211) A third actor tightly linked to a specific character, [Paul Reubens](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Reubens "Paul Reubens") as [Pee-wee Herman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pee-wee_Herman "Pee-wee Herman"), is also honored, although in this case the star lists only the character, not the actor.[[217]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-223) [The Muppets](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Muppets "The Muppets") collectively have three stars, one for the group and the others for [Kermit the Frog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kermit_the_Frog "Kermit the Frog") and [Big Bird](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Big_Bird "Big Bird"); Muppets' creator [Jim Henson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jim_Henson "Jim Henson") has a star as well.[[218]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-224)
#### Shared names
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=24 "Edit section: Shared names")]
[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/59/Star_%283665961775%29.jpg/250px-Star_%283665961775%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Star_\(3665961775\).jpg)
Radio personality [Michael Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_\(radio_commentator\) "Michael Jackson \(radio commentator\)")'s star after [the 2009 death of the musician of the same name](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Michael_Jackson "Death of Michael Jackson")
[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Michael_Jackson_Star.JPG/330px-Michael_Jackson_Star.JPG?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Michael_Jackson_Star.JPG)
Musician and recording artist [Michael Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson "Michael Jackson")'s star about two weeks after [his death](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Michael_Jackson "Death of Michael Jackson")
Two pairs of stars share identical names representing different people. There are two Harrison Ford stars, one for [the silent-film actor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harrison_Ford_\(silent_film_actor\) "Harrison Ford \(silent film actor\)") and the other for [the more recent actor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harrison_Ford "Harrison Ford"), and two Michael Jackson stars, one for [the pop singer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson "Michael Jackson") and the other for [the radio personality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_\(radio_commentator\) "Michael Jackson \(radio commentator\)").[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4) After [singer Michael Jackson's death](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Michael_Jackson "Death of Michael Jackson") in 2009, many fans mistakenly paid tribute at the radio personality's star, the singer's being inaccessible due to the [premiere](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Premiere "Premiere") of _[Brüno](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Br%C3%BCno "Brüno")_.[[219]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-225)
#### Groups and pairings
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=25 "Edit section: Groups and pairings")]
[![The six members of Village People, each dressed in character \(police officer, soldier, biker, construction worker, Native American, and cowboy\) hold Walk of Fame plaques as they pose for a picture by their Walk of Fame star. A crowd is behind them.](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/4d/VPStarfront.jpg/250px-VPStarfront.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:VPStarfront.jpg)[Village People](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Village_People "Village People") at their star unveiling in 2008
Some stars honor groups, not individuals. Dozens of music groups have been honored, including [The Beatles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Beatles "The Beatles"), [The Beach Boys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Beach_Boys "The Beach Boys"), [The Jackson 5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Jackson_5 "The Jackson 5"), [The Supremes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Supremes "The Supremes"), [Queen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Queen_\(band\) "Queen \(band\)"), [Village People](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Village_People "Village People"), [Green Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Day "Green Day"), [Backstreet Boys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Backstreet_Boys "Backstreet Boys"), and [Destiny’s Child](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Destiny%E2%80%99s_Child "Destiny’s Child").[[220]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-226) Sports teams are also honored, including the [Harlem Globetrotters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harlem_Globetrotters "Harlem Globetrotters") and [Los Angeles Dodgers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Dodgers "Los Angeles Dodgers").[[221]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-227)[[222]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-228)
Other stars honor professional pairings rather than individuals. Examples include radio co-hosts [Al Lohman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Lohman "Al Lohman") & [Roger Barkley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Barkley "Roger Barkley"), [Ken Minyard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ken_Minyard "Ken Minyard") & [Bob Arthur](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bob_Arthur "Bob Arthur"), [Mark & Brian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_&_Brian "Mark & Brian"), and [Mark & Kim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_&_Kim "Mark & Kim");[[223]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-229) live performers [Siegfried & Roy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siegfried_&_Roy "Siegfried & Roy")[[224]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-230) and [Jan & Mickey Rooney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jan_&_Mickey_Rooney "Jan & Mickey Rooney");[[225]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-janandmickey-231) television writers [Sid & Marty Krofft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sid_&_Marty_Krofft "Sid & Marty Krofft");[[226]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-232) actors [Mary-Kate & Ashley Olsen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mary-Kate_&_Ashley_Olsen "Mary-Kate & Ashley Olsen");[[227]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-233) and music duos [Sonny & Cher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sonny_&_Cher "Sonny & Cher"),[[228]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-234) [BeBe & CeCe Winans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BeBe_&_CeCe_Winans "BeBe & CeCe Winans"),[[229]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-235) [Steve Lawrence & Eydie Gorme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steve_and_Eydie "Steve and Eydie"),[[230]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-236) and [Daryl Hall & John Oates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daryl_Hall_&_John_Oates "Daryl Hall & John Oates").[[231]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-237) Mary-Kate and Ashley Olsen are the only twins to share a star.[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4)
#### Repeat selections and entries
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=26 "Edit section: Repeat selections and entries")]
[Apollo 11](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_11 "Apollo 11") astronauts [Neil Armstrong](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neil_Armstrong "Neil Armstrong"), [Buzz Aldrin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buzz_Aldrin "Buzz Aldrin"), and [Michael Collins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Collins_\(astronaut\) "Michael Collins \(astronaut\)") share four monuments, one on each corner of [Hollywood and Vine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_and_Vine "Hollywood and Vine"), all in the television category.[[26]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-military-28)[[27]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-specialapollo-29)[[28]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LATApollo-30) Similarly, [George Eastman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Eastman "George Eastman") was given two stars in the same category for the same achievement: the invention of [roll film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roll_film "Roll film").[[232]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-238)
[Charlie Chaplin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charlie_Chaplin "Charlie Chaplin") is the only honoree to be selected twice for the same star. He was unanimously voted into the initial group in 1956, but the Selection Committee ultimately excluded him, reportedly due to questions regarding his morals[[233]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-chaplin_exclusion-239) but also possibly due to his political views.[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195) Chaplin was re-selected and added in 1972;[[12]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LATabout-12) he received an honorary [Academy Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award "Academy Award") that same year.[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4) [Paul Robeson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Robeson "Paul Robeson") was another whose views reportedly contributed to the committee's decision against awarding him a star;[[234]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-240) however, the resulting outcry was so intense that the decision was reversed and Robeson was awarded a star one year later, in 1979.[[235]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Qualles-241)[[236]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Jet78-242)[[237]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Jet79-243)
### Organizations
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=27 "Edit section: Organizations")]
To be considered for an Award of Excellence, commercial organizations must have a Hollywood connection and at least fifty years of service in show business. While not technically on the Walk, as the [city of Los Angeles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Los_Angeles "Government of Los Angeles") prohibits corporate names on sidewalks, these stars are installed on private property adjacent to it.[[5]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-disneylandorg-5) [The Walt Disney Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Walt_Disney_Company "The Walt Disney Company") was the first company to receive an Award of Excellence, for [Disneyland](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disneyland "Disneyland"),[[238]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-244) while [Musso and Frank](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Musso_and_Frank "Musso and Frank") was the first restaurant and [Chevy Suburban](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chevy_Suburban "Chevy Suburban") the first inanimate object to receive these awards.[[239]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-245)[[240]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-246) Other commercial organizations with Awards of Excellence include _[The Hollywood Reporter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Hollywood_Reporter "The Hollywood Reporter")_ , [KTLA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KTLA "KTLA"), [Hanna-Barbera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hanna-Barbera "Hanna-Barbera"), [Los Angeles Dodgers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Dodgers "Los Angeles Dodgers"), _[Variety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Variety_\(magazine\) "Variety \(magazine\)")_ , and [Victoria's Secret Angels](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victoria's_Secret_Angels "Victoria's Secret Angels").[[5]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-disneylandorg-5)[[12]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LATabout-12)[[241]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-latimes_orgs-247)
[![Star and background that are same size, colors, and design as those of the Walk of Fame. The star reads "L.A.P.D. Hollywood" and under the text is a Hollywood Division police badge. Outside the star, on the background, are eight plaques: seven display police officer names and dates, while the eighth explains the Award. The background is surrounded by concrete](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d2/Photo_wof_lapd_hollywood-01.JPG/250px-Photo_wof_lapd_hollywood-01.JPG?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Photo_wof_lapd_hollywood-01.JPG)[Los Angeles Police Department Hollywood Division](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Police_Department "Los Angeles Police Department")'s Award of Excellence in 2001
Non-commercial organizations honored with Awards of Excellence include the [Los Angeles Fire Department](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Fire_Department "Los Angeles Fire Department") (LAFD), [Los Angeles Police Department](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Police_Department "Los Angeles Police Department") (LAPD) Hollywood Division, [The Recording Academy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Recording_Academy "The Recording Academy"), [Screen Actors Guild](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Screen_Actors_Guild "Screen Actors Guild"), and the Walk of Fame itself.[[241]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-latimes_orgs-247)[[242]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LAFD-248) The LAPD Hollywood Division star is accompanied by the names of several division officers killed in the line of duty,[[243]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-249)[[244]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-250) while LAFD received their award the same year as the [January 2025 Southern California wildfires](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/January_2025_Southern_California_wildfires "January 2025 Southern California wildfires"); the latter was suggested by an eighth grader in [Orange, Connecticut](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orange,_Connecticut "Orange, Connecticut"), as part of an assignment to write a letter about heroes.[[242]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LAFD-248)
### Errors
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=28 "Edit section: Errors")]
Several stars have been unveiled with the wrong name. Film and television actor [Don Haggerty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Don_Haggerty "Don Haggerty")'s star originally displayed the first name "Dan"; the name was corrected and then, years later, television actor [Dan Haggerty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dan_Haggerty "Dan Haggerty") (no relation to Don) received his own star.[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4)[[245]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-haggerty-251) Other stars that bear the wrong name include [Thomas Kane Tully](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tom_Tully "Tom Tully")'s, which reads "Thomas L. Tully",[[246]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-252) and [Mauritz Stiller](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mauritz_Stiller "Mauritz Stiller")'s prior to 1988, when it read "Maurice Diller".[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4)[[247]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-253)
In addition to incorrect names, some honorees' names have been misspelled. In 2010, [Julia Louis-Dreyfus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Julia_Louis-Dreyfus "Julia Louis-Dreyfus")'s star was constructed reading "Julia Luis Dreyfus".[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4)[[248]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-254) The actress reportedly thought the mistake was hysterical and wanted it to remain, but it was corrected.[[249]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-255) Similarly, [Dick Van Dyke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dick_Van_Dyke "Dick Van Dyke")'s star misspelled his last name as "Vandyke" before it was rectified,[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4) and several names remain misspelled, including [Lotte Lehmann](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lotte_Lehmann "Lotte Lehmann") (misspelled as "Lottie"), [Merian C. Cooper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Merian_C._Cooper "Merian C. Cooper") ("Meriam"), and [Auguste Lumière](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Auguste_Lumi%C3%A8re "Auguste Lumière") ("August").[[250]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-256) Some stars also contain emblems that do not match the category the honoree was honored in, including [Carmen Miranda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carmen_Miranda "Carmen Miranda"), who was honored in the motion picture category but her star bears the television emblem, and [Larry King](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Larry_King "Larry King"), who was honored in the television category but his star bears a motion picture emblem.[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-ign-4)
### Controversial
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=29 "Edit section: Controversial")]
Several honorees have resulted in controversy, some immediately when honored and others later. [Charlie Chaplin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charlie_Chaplin "Charlie Chaplin")'s initial selection was so controversial (reportedly due to questions about his morals but also possibly due to his political views) that it was revoked,[[233]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-chaplin_exclusion-239)[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195) and today his inclusion would be questioned due to his multiple relationships with minors.[[251]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-metoo-257) [Marlon Brando](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marlon_Brando "Marlon Brando")'s inclusion received backlash following reports of [anti-Semitism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Semitism "Anti-Semitism"),[[73]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-reilly-76) as did [Gal Gadot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gal_Gadot "Gal Gadot")'s due to her outspoken support of the [Israeli military](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_military "Israeli military").[[252]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-258)[[253]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-gadot-259)
Numerous individuals and organizations have called or petitioned for the removal of [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump")'s star, including local residents,[[254]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-260) Latino advocacy groups,[[255]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-hreporter-261) and [West Hollywood City Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Hollywood,_California#Government "West Hollywood, California"), the latter of which stated that "the Hollywood Walk of Fame is an honor. When one belittles and attacks minorities, immigrants, Muslims, people with disabilities or women – the honor no longer exists."[[256]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-262)[[257]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-263) In 2016, an online petition to have Trump's star removed received more than 40,000 signatures.[[258]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpstar-264) Others have also come to the star's defense.[[259]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpsupport-265)[[260]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumprally-266)
[Spade Cooley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spade_Cooley "Spade Cooley") is the only convicted murderer honored by the Walk.[[89]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-walkofinfamy-93) He is not the only murderer though, as [Gig Young](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gig_Young "Gig Young") is known to have murdered his wife. Young was never convicted, however, as he committed suicide shortly after.[[255]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-hreporter-261) Other controversial criminal-related stars include those of accused [domestic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Domestic_abuse "Domestic abuse") and [sexual abusers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_abuse "Sexual abuse"), including accused [rapists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rapists "Rapists").[[251]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-metoo-257)[[255]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-hreporter-261) The [MeToo movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MeToo_movement "MeToo movement") saw concerted efforts to have several of these honorees removed, most notably [Bill Cosby](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bill_Cosby "Bill Cosby") both before and after his [sexual assault conviction that was later overturned](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trial_of_Bill_Cosby "Trial of Bill Cosby").[[73]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-reilly-76)[[251]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-metoo-257)[[261]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-267) Similar removal campaigns were held against [Sean Combs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sean_Combs "Sean Combs").[[93]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-cananyone-98)
Some honorees are controversial because they are considered to have lost their fame, never been famous enough in the first place, not done enough to earn their star or fame, lacked talent, or otherwise been unworthy. Honorees that have been described in this manner include [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears "Britney Spears"), [Gal Gadot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gal_Gadot "Gal Gadot"), [Olive Borden](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olive_Borden "Olive Borden"), [Louise Fazenda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Louise_Fazenda "Louise Fazenda"), [David Spade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Spade "David Spade"), [Vanna White](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vanna_White "Vanna White"), and [Michael Bolton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Bolton "Michael Bolton").[[29]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-starplaces-31)[[262]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-268)[[263]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-269)[[264]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-270)
### Rejected
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=30 "Edit section: Rejected")]
The Chamber rejects about 85–90% of the nominations it receives each year.[[Note 7]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-271) Rejections are not made public; however, after [Kim Kardashian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kim_Kardashian "Kim Kardashian") publicly stated her desire for a star, the Chamber clarified that her nomination would be rejected as the Walk does not honor [reality stars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reality_television "Reality television") unless they have been nominated for or won an [Oscar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Awards "Academy Awards"), [Emmy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emmy "Emmy"), [Grammy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grammy "Grammy"), or other major award given for a performance.[[265]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-272)[[266]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-273) In another instance, when asked if the Walk would ever honor reality stars, the Chamber responded "Hell to the no."[[267]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-274) Another known rejection was ["Weird Al" Yankovic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/"Weird_Al"_Yankovic ""Weird Al" Yankovic"), who was rejected eleven times before being accepted in 2017.[[268]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-275) A campaign to get [King Kong](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/King_Kong "King Kong") a star was unsuccessful as well.[[71]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-garrison-74)
Numerous individuals have been selected for the Walk but rejected the honor, including [Leonardo DiCaprio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leonardo_DiCaprio "Leonardo DiCaprio"), [Julia Roberts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Julia_Roberts "Julia Roberts"), [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna "Madonna"), [Bruce Springsteen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bruce_Springsteen "Bruce Springsteen"), [Denzel Washington](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Denzel_Washington "Denzel Washington"), [Clint Eastwood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clint_Eastwood "Clint Eastwood"), and [Prince](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prince_\(musician\) "Prince \(musician\)"),[[61]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-janetlee-64)[[69]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-prince-72)[[269]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-276) the latter of whom rejected it twice.[[69]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-prince-72) Others, including [Whitney Houston](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Whitney_Houston "Whitney Houston"), [Al Pacino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Pacino "Al Pacino"), [George Clooney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Clooney "George Clooney"), and [Will Smith](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Will_Smith "Will Smith"), de facto rejected their star by never scheduling an unveiling.[[270]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-277)[[271]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-278)
## Visitors
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=31 "Edit section: Visitors")]
Hugo Martín wrote in the _[Los Angeles Times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Times "Los Angeles Times")_ that the Walk of Fame has been a significant contributor towards making tourism the largest industry in [Los Angeles County](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_County "Los Angeles County").[[272]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Martin1-279) It attracts about ten million visitors annually, which according to a 2003 report by market research firm NFO Plog Research is more than the [Sunset Strip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunset_Strip "Sunset Strip"), [Grauman's Chinese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grauman's_Chinese "Grauman's Chinese"), [Queen Mary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RMS_Queen_Mary "RMS Queen Mary"), and the [Los Angeles County Museum of Art](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_County_Museum_of_Art "Los Angeles County Museum of Art").[[272]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Martin1-279) In 2025, [U.S. News](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._News "U.S. News") ranked the Walk as the third best attraction in Los Angeles, after [Griffith Observatory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Griffith_Observatory "Griffith Observatory") and the [Getty Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Getty_Center "Getty Center").[[273]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-280) It can be accessed via car,[[274]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-281) bike,[[275]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-bike-282)[[276]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-construction-283) bus,[[276]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-construction-283) tour bus,[[277]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-284) and two subway stations: [Hollywood/Highland](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood/Highland_station "Hollywood/Highland station")[[278]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-285) and [Hollywood/Vine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood/Vine_station "Hollywood/Vine station").[[275]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-bike-282)
Despite the Walk's popularity and prestige, visitors are often underwhelmed[[279]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-286) due to its perceived dirtiness, grittiness, and problems with homelessness and crime.[[280]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-walkoffamebad-287) The Walk has repeatedly been called the "Walk of Shame"[[281]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-288) and "the world's worst tourist attraction";[[282]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-289) it has also been described as "cheesy",[[283]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-290) "tacky",[[284]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-291) a "promotional gambit",[[71]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-garrison-74) and a [tourist trap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tourist_trap "Tourist trap").[[285]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-292)
### Street vendors and performers
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=32 "Edit section: Street vendors and performers")]
[![Two guitarists play on one side of a row of Walk of Fame stars, Hollywood Boulevard behind them, while pedestrians walk by on the other. Cars, including a blue and yellow taxi, and buildings can be seen in the background.](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/45/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame.jpg/250px-Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame.jpg)[Buskers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buskers "Buskers") performing on the Walk
The Walk of Fame is one of Los Angeles's most popular locations for street vendors. In 2018, street vending was banned, but the ban was rescinded in 2024.[[286]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-293) Vendors commonly sell merchandise or [bacon-wrapped hot dogs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Danger_dog "Danger dog").[[287]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-294)[[288]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-295)
[Buskers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buskers "Buskers"), musicians distributing albums, and other [street](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Street_performers "Street performers") and [costumed performers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Costumed_performer "Costumed performer") also frequent the Walk, particularly outside [Grauman's Chinese Theater](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grauman's_Chinese_Theater "Grauman's Chinese Theater") and [Madame Tussauds Hollywood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madame_Tussauds_Hollywood "Madame Tussauds Hollywood"). In 2016, [City Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_City_Council "Los Angeles City Council") considered limiting the number permitted in the area, as many visitors report negative experiences due to the performers' rude and aggressive behavior. The performers have also been known to get into altercations with each other.[[289]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-296)[[290]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-297)
### Unveilings
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=33 "Edit section: Unveilings")]
[![A large crowd of onlookers behind barriers surrounding a platform, with Scarlett Johansson in a black and white dress and emcee Leron Gubler in a black suit on the platform and smiling to the crowd](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/f0/Scarlett_Johansson_%40_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_02.jpg/250px-Scarlett_Johansson_%40_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_02.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Scarlett_Johansson_@_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_02.jpg)A portion of the crowd at [Scarlett Johansson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scarlett_Johansson "Scarlett Johansson")'s star unveiling in 2012
Star unveilings are free to attend;[[11]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-WOFFAQ-11) are almost always paid for by a studio, network, record label, or other corporate patron;[[84]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-baum-87) usually coincide with a new release from the honoree;[[84]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-baum-87) and attract crowds in the dozens, hundreds, or thousands. [Michael Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson "Michael Jackson")'s unveiling in 1984 set a record for the highest number of attendees, with 5,000;[[47]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-itisnteasy-50)[[291]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-298) however, the spokeswoman for the Hollywood Chamber of Commerce stated that the 4,500 attendees at [Selena Quintanilla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selena_Quintanilla "Selena Quintanilla")'s unveiling in 2017 was a record, surpassing the previous high of 4,000 at [Vicente Fernandez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vicente_Fernandez "Vicente Fernandez")'s unveiling in 1998.[[292]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-299)[[293]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-300) [Anthony Anderson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anthony_Anderson "Anthony Anderson")'s unveiling, which occurred during the [COVID-19 pandemic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic "COVID-19 pandemic"), had a notably low attendance of 25.[[61]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-janetlee-64)
In addition to fans, unveilings are often attended by celebrity entourages.[[294]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-301) Some unveilings also include performances, for example, [Home Free](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Home_Free_\(group\) "Home Free \(group\)") sang at [Don McLean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Don_McLean "Don McLean")'s unveiling,[[295]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-302) a children's choir sang at [Andrea Bocelli](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrea_Bocelli "Andrea Bocelli")'s,[[35]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-martinez-37) a [brass band](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brass_band "Brass band") played at [Winnie the Pooh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winnie_the_Pooh_\(Disney_character\) "Winnie the Pooh \(Disney character\)")'s,[[296]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-303) and [marching bands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marching_band "Marching band") have played at several unveilings, including those for [Bob Hope](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bob_Hope "Bob Hope"),[[297]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-304) [Ringo Starr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ringo_Starr "Ringo Starr"),[[298]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-305) [Jerry Buss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jerry_Buss "Jerry Buss"),[[299]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-306) [Gary Collins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gary_Collins_\(actor\) "Gary Collins \(actor\)"),[[300]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-307) the [Munchkins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Munchkins "Munchkins"),[[205]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-coleman-210) and the [Red Hot Chili Peppers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Hot_Chili_Peppers "Red Hot Chili Peppers").[[301]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-308)
### Tributes
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=34 "Edit section: Tributes")]
[![Robin Williams's star with flowers \(scattered, potted, and bouquets\), photographs, paper, and an award that reads "Brightest Star in the Comedy Galaxy" covering all but his name and the surrounding charcoal terrazzo](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8d/Robin_Williams_star%2C_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_am_28.08.14.jpg/250px-Robin_Williams_star%2C_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_am_28.08.14.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Robin_Williams_star,_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_am_28.08.14.jpg)[Robin Williams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robin_Williams "Robin Williams")'s star shortly after his death in 2014
The [Hollywood Chamber of Commerce](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Chamber_of_Commerce "Hollywood Chamber of Commerce") has adopted the tradition of placing flower wreaths at the stars of newly deceased honorees; as a result, these stars often become memorial and vigil sites.[[302]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-wreath-309) Fans often show respect by leaving flowers and messages,[[303]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-310) and some continue to do so on anniversary remembrances.[[85]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-starpolishers-88) Others show support in other ways; for example, [Julio Iglesias](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Julio_Iglesias "Julio Iglesias")'s star was cleaned and polished once a month by a group of elderly women[[304]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-Halpern-311) and [Barry Manilow](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barry_Manilow "Barry Manilow")'s star was buffed by a mother-daughter pair once a week.[[85]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-starpolishers-88)
### Rallies, protests, and vandalism
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=35 "Edit section: Rallies, protests, and vandalism")]
Walk of Fame stars are sometimes used as sites for rallies or protests.[[305]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-USCcamstudy-312) Often, the protests occur as vandalism, which has ranged from profanity and political statements to damage with heavy tools.[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195)[[306]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-313) In 2005, [closed circuit surveillance cameras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Closed-circuit_television "Closed-circuit television") were installed on Hollywood between La Brea and Vine to discourage these activities.[[305]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-USCcamstudy-312) Additionally, if an honoree has a large enough scandal, police will increase their presence around that person's star to deter vandalism.[[251]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-metoo-257)
[Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump")'s star, obtained for his work on the [Miss Universe pageant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miss_Universe_pageant "Miss Universe pageant") and _[The Apprentice](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Apprentice_\(American_TV_series\) "The Apprentice \(American TV series\)")_ ,[[251]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-metoo-257)[[307]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-314) has been the site of numerous protests and rallies; it has also been vandalized multiple times. During the [2016 presidential election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_United_States_presidential_election "2016 United States presidential election"), a [service dog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Service_dog "Service dog")'s owner had the dog defecate on Trump's star, which was then posted on [Twitter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twitter "Twitter").[[258]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpstar-264) Shortly after, the star was placed behind a miniature [border wall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Border_wall "Border wall"),[[308]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-baby-315)[[309]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpVF-316) and in October, James Otis, an heir to the [Otis Elevator Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Otis_Worldwide "Otis Worldwide") fortune,[[310]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-317)[[311]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-318) used a pickaxe and sledgehammer to destroy the star.[[312]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-319) Otis readily admitted to the vandalism[[313]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-320) and was fined $4,000 ($5,366in 2025) and sentenced to twenty days of work for the [California Department of Transportation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/California_Department_of_Transportation "California Department of Transportation") and three years' probation.[[314]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-321)[[315]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-322)[[316]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trump2019-323) The star was repaired and continued to serve as the site of rallies and protests throughout and after the election.[[260]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumprally-266)[[309]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpVF-316)[[317]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-324)[[318]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-325)
[![Construction workers in high-vis yellow vests work with trash bags and a wheelbarrow on the sidewalk around a recently poured terrazzo pad and unidentifiable star. Police officers in front of caution tape and pedestrians behind caution tape watch. Disney, Ghirardelli, and TMZ banners are visible across the street.](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/cd/Donald_Trump%27s_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_Star_being_repaired.jpg/250px-Donald_Trump%27s_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_Star_being_repaired.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Donald_Trump's_Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame_Star_being_repaired.jpg)[Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump")'s star under repair after vandalism in 2018
In 2017, Trump's star was covered in protest stickers shortly before his inauguration and also during [Los Angeles Pride](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Pride "Los Angeles Pride"). A golden toilet with the words "Take a Trump" was also placed beside Trump's star that year.[[309]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpVF-316) Further vandalism occurred in 2018 when Trump's star was destroyed again, this time by Austin Clay.[[319]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-326) Clay later surrendered to police, after which he was sentenced to one day in jail, twenty days of community service, and three years of probation. He was also ordered to undergo psychological counseling and pay $9,404.46 ($12,058in 2025) in restitution.[[320]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-327) Later in 2018, the star was "guarded" by fake [Russian soldiers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_soldier "Russian soldier"),[[321]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-328) placed behind bars,[[309]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpVF-316)[[322]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-329) and defaced with [swastikas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swastika "Swastika"), fake blood, and other graffiti.[[73]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-reilly-76)[[323]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-330) It was also the site of multiple fights[[324]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-331) and elsewhere on the Walk, dozens of blank stars were vandalized in support of Trump's star.[[259]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trumpsupport-265) Trump's star was vandalized multiple times again in 2019 and 2020, including with a [pickaxe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pickaxe "Pickaxe"), with [dog feces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dog_feces "Dog feces"), and with [spray-paint](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spray-paint "Spray-paint").[[316]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-trump2019-323)[[325]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-332)[[326]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-333)[[327]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-334) The star was vandalized again in 2026.[[328]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-335)
Other politicians' stars have not seen the same level of protests or vandalism;[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195) however, [Ronald Reagan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ronald_Reagan "Ronald Reagan")'s star was the site of a 1981 gathering to request signatures petitioning him to keep [leopards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leopard "Leopard") on the [endangered species list](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Endangered_species_list "Endangered species list").[[329]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-336) The star was also peed on for [David Bowie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Bowie "David Bowie")'s _[Day-In Day-Out](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Day-In_Day-Out "Day-In Day-Out")_ music video; however, the moment was removed so that [MTV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV "MTV") would accept the video into their rotation.[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195)
Protests and vandalism of non-politicians' stars also occur. In 2009, [Sharon Stone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sharon_Stone "Sharon Stone")'s, [Mary-Kate and Ashley Olsen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mary-Kate_and_Ashley_Olsen "Mary-Kate and Ashley Olsen")'s, and [Aretha Franklin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aretha_Franklin "Aretha Franklin")'s stars had the words "Old Fur Hag", "Fur Hags", and "Fur Hag" written on them, presumably in protest of them wearing [fur clothing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fur_clothing "Fur clothing").[[330]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-337) In 2015, [Sofia Vergara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sofia_Vergara "Sofia Vergara")'s star was graffitied in reference to her use of [frozen embryos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frozen_embryo "Frozen embryo").[[331]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-NotTheOnlyOne-338)[[332]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-339) In 2017, [Bob Marley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bob_Marley "Bob Marley")'s star was damaged by a heavy object[[333]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-340) and protesters also gathered around [Kevin Spacey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kevin_Spacey "Kevin Spacey")'s star to protest [predatory culture in Hollywood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_abuse_in_the_American_film_industry "Sexual abuse in the American film industry").[[334]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-341) In 2018, [Jennifer Lopez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jennifer_Lopez "Jennifer Lopez"), [Ellen DeGeneres](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ellen_DeGeneres "Ellen DeGeneres"), [Pharrell Williams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pharrell_Williams "Pharrell Williams"), and [Michael Bublé](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Bubl%C3%A9 "Michael Bublé")'s stars were sprayed with black paint,[[335]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-342) and in 2024, [Selena Quintanilla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selena_Quintanilla "Selena Quintanilla") and [Jenni Rivera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jenni_Rivera "Jenni Rivera")'s stars were covered in black paint twice within 24 hours.[[336]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-343) In 2025, [Gal Gadot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gal_Gadot "Gal Gadot")'s star was vandalized in reference to the [Israeli military](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_military "Israeli military"),[[253]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-gadot-259) and [Dolly Parton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dolly_Parton "Dolly Parton")'s star was vandalized shortly after her death in 2026.[[337]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-344) [Bill Cosby](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bill_Cosby "Bill Cosby")'s star has been vandalized multiple times, including in 2014 and 2018,[[331]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-NotTheOnlyOne-338) and in 2015 protestors (including [Lili Bernard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lili_Bernard "Lili Bernard") and [Victoria Valentino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victoria_Valentino "Victoria Valentino")) used the site to protest [rape limitation laws](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Statute_of_limitations "Statute of limitations") in California.[[338]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-345)[[339]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-346) [Radio commentator Michael Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_\(radio_commentator\) "Michael Jackson \(radio commentator\)")'s star has also been defaced (presumably, it was mistaken for [the singer's star with the same name](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson "Michael Jackson")), after which [Paris Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paris_Jackson "Paris Jackson") cleaned it up.[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195)[[331]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-NotTheOnlyOne-338)
Not all Walk of Fame protests are directed at individuals. For example, in 1988, activists sledgehammered the Walk's curb in support of the [Americans with Disabilities Act](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Americans_with_Disabilities_Act_of_1990 "Americans with Disabilities Act of 1990");[[340]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-347) in 2014, protestors wrote messages on the Walk in response to the decision not to indict [Eric Garner's killer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Killing_of_Eric_Garner "Killing of Eric Garner");[[341]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-348) in 2017, [MeToo movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MeToo_movement "MeToo movement") supporters marched along the Walk;[[342]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-349) in 2020, the Walk was the gathering point for an estimated 25,000 [Black Lives Matter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_Lives_Matter "Black Lives Matter") protestors;[[343]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-350) and in 2022, activists held an abortion rights rally on the Walk.[[344]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-351)
Finally, not all vandalism on the Walk is a form of protest. For example, [John Lennon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Lennon "John Lennon")'s star was defaced with several non-negative messages in 2013,[[331]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-NotTheOnlyOne-338) [Hugh Hefner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hugh_Hefner "Hugh Hefner")'s star was defaced with a blue crown and the letters "RIP" shortly after his death in 2017,[[331]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-NotTheOnlyOne-338) and [Prince](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prince_\(singer\) "Prince \(singer\)")'s likeness was drawn on a blank star after his death in 2016; the Chamber allowed the latter to remain for more than a week so people could use it to mourn.[[69]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-prince-72)[[73]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-reilly-76)
## Influence
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=36 "Edit section: Influence")]
