[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1b/Pola_Negri_gwiazda_Lodz.jpg/120px-Pola_Negri_gwiazda_Lodz.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Pola_Negri_gwiazda_Lodz.jpg)
[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/77/Sheryl_Cruz_Eastwood_City_Walk_of_Fame_star.jpg/120px-Sheryl_Cruz_Eastwood_City_Walk_of_Fame_star.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Sheryl_Cruz_Eastwood_City_Walk_of_Fame_star.jpg)
Stars in (left to right, top to bottom): Almeria, Berlin, Hong Kong, Las Vegas, Łódź, and Quezon City
The Hollywood Walk of Fame has inspired walks throughout the world. Other walks that either cite Hollywood's as their inspiration or claim to be their country's equivalent to Hollywood's include those in [Almeria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Almeria_Walk_of_Fame "Almeria Walk of Fame");[[345]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-352) [Berlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boulevard_der_Stars "Boulevard der Stars");[[346]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-353) [Cambridge, Massachusetts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Entrepreneur_Walk_of_Fame "Entrepreneur Walk of Fame");[[347]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-354) [Hong Kong](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avenue_of_Stars,_Hong_Kong "Avenue of Stars, Hong Kong");[[348]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-355) [Las Vegas, Nevada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Las_Vegas_Walk_of_Stars "Las Vegas Walk of Stars");[[349]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-356) [Łódź](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piotrkowska_Street#%C5%81%C3%B3d%C5%BA_Walk_of_Fame "Piotrkowska Street");[[350]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-357) [London](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avenue_of_Stars,_London "Avenue of Stars, London");[[351]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-358) [Mexico City](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paseo_de_las_Luminarias "Paseo de las Luminarias");[[352]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-359) [Mumbai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walk_of_the_Stars "Walk of the Stars");[[353]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-360) [Quezon City](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eastwood_City_Walk_of_Fame "Eastwood City Walk of Fame");[[354]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-361) [Sydney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sydney_Writers_Walk "Sydney Writers Walk");[[355]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-362) [Wembley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wembley_Arena#Square_of_Fame "Wembley Arena");[[356]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-363) and many more. Los Angeles alone has several walks inspired by Hollywood's[[357]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-364) and elsewhere in California, the creators of San Francisco's [Rainbow Honor Walk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rainbow_Honor_Walk "Rainbow Honor Walk") and [Walk of Game](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walk_of_Game "Walk of Game") have both cited the Hollywood Walk of Fame as their inspiration.[[358]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-365)[[359]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-366)
The Walk has also influenced protestors, not just in Hollywood, but elsewhere. Examples include a 2020 protest in [Mumbai, India](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mumbai,_India "Mumbai, India"),[[360]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-367)[[361]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-368) and a 2026 [Jeffrey Epstein](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeffrey_Epstein "Jeffrey Epstein") protest in [Washington, D.C.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Washington_D.C. "Washington D.C.")[[362]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-369)[[363]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-370)
### In media
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=37 "Edit section: In media")]
Notable films that feature the Hollywood Walk of Fame include _[Pretty Woman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pretty_Woman "Pretty Woman")_ , where [streetwalkers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Street_prostitution "Street prostitution") use the names on several stars to describe where they streetwalk;[[190]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-fallen-195) _[The Substance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Substance "The Substance")_ , which begins and ends on the main character's fictional star;[[364]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-371) _[Harry and Tonto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harry_and_Tonto "Harry and Tonto")_ , where the titular characters are picked up at the Walk;[[365]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-372) _[Inland Empire](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inland_Empire_\(film\) "Inland Empire \(film\)")_ , where the main character vomits blood on the Walk;[[366]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-373) and _[The Fanatic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Fanatic_\(film\) "The Fanatic \(film\)")_ , where the main character is a [busker](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Busker "Busker") on the Walk.[[367]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-374) _[Jimmy Kimmel Live!](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jimmy_Kimmel_Live! "Jimmy Kimmel Live!")_ has done skits on the Walk, including having [Mark Hamill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_Hamill "Mark Hamill") pretend to remove [Jimmy Kimmel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jimmy_Kimmel "Jimmy Kimmel")'s star to make room for his own[[368]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-375) and having Hamill stand beside his star to see if anyone would recognize him.[[369]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-376) The music video for [Miley Cyrus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miley_Cyrus "Miley Cyrus")'s "Walk of Fame" features the Walk,[[370]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-377) [The Kinks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Kinks "The Kinks")'s _[Celluloid Heroes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Celluloid_Heroes "Celluloid Heroes")_ was inspired by and also mentions the Walk,[[371]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-378) and [Sofie Royer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sofie_Royer "Sofie Royer") has a song titled "Hollywood Walk of Fame".[[372]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-379) Street photographer [Garry Winogrand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Garry_Winogrand "Garry Winogrand") has also featured the Walk of Fame in his work.[[373]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-380)[[374]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-381)

## Notes
[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit&section=39 "Edit section: Notes")]
  1. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-16) Recording is sometimes referred to as music.[[15]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-michigan-15)
  2. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-20) As of October16,2025[[update]](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit)[[18]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-19)
  3. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-38) She was reported to have been in charge for 25 years as of 2012[[update]](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit)[[29]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-starplaces-31) and 35 years as of 2023[[update]](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame&action=edit)[[35]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-martinez-37)
  4. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-89) The group was formed about 15 years before 1995.[[85]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-starpolishers-88)
  5. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-96) The Walk is a [Los Angeles Historic-Cultural Monument](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Historic-Cultural_Monument "Los Angeles Historic-Cultural Monument")[[90]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-LAHCM-94) and although it is not registered in the [National Register of Historic Places](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Register_of_Historic_Places "National Register of Historic Places"), it is mentioned as an element of "fine urban design" in the [Hollywood Boulevard Commercial and Entertainment District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Boulevard_Commercial_and_Entertainment_District "Hollywood Boulevard Commercial and Entertainment District")'s listing.[[91]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-HBCED-95)
  6. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-218) Many sources list [Mickey Mouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mickey_Mouse "Mickey Mouse") as the first fictional character to be honored by, added to, or to receive a star on the Walk of Fame, including the _[New York Times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_York_Times "New York Times")_ ,[[209]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-214) _[Chicago Tribune](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chicago_Tribune "Chicago Tribune")_ ,[[210]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-215) _[The Independent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Independent "The Independent")_ ,[[211]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-216) _[Entertainment Weekly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Entertainment_Weekly "Entertainment Weekly")_ ,[[205]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-coleman-210) and the _[Guinness Book of World Records](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guinness_Book_of_World_Records "Guinness Book of World Records")_.[[212]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-217)
  7. [↑](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_ref-271) According to the Walk's FAQ, each year an average of 30 new stars are selected from 200–300 nominations.[[11]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-WOFFAQ-11) However, according to the Nomination FAQ, an average of 24–30 are selected from 200 nominations.[[70]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_Walk_of_Fame#cite_note-WOFFAQforNoms-73)
